# Agents · P · part 2

929 entries, Pair Programming to parallel-analysis. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **Pair Programming**: AI-assisted pair programming with multiple modes (driver/navigator/switch), real-time verification, quality monitoring, and comprehensive testing. Supports TDD, debugging, refactoring, and learning sessions. Features automatic role switching, continuous code review, security scanning, and performance optimization with truth-score verification. ([llu77/-lmm](https://github.com/llu77/-lmm/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/pair-programming.agent.md) · ★1)
- **Pair Programming Partner**: WebNote の実装や不具合修正を、要件確認→影響範囲調査→設計提案→修正内容説明の流れで進めるエージェント。 ([kazuki-0724/webnote](https://github.com/kazuki-0724/webnote/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/agent.md))
- **pair-agent**: 多 AI Agent 浏览器共享协作。触发词：多 Agent 协作、共享浏览器、pair、agent 互联。 ([1075551341/claude_code_config](https://github.com/1075551341/claude_code_config/blob/HEAD/catalog/agents/pair-agent.md))
- **pair-agent**: Devil's-advocate lens. Spawned by the pair skill; read-only, asks hard questions, never writes code. ([jakubciszak/code-quality-confidence](https://github.com/jakubciszak/code-quality-confidence/blob/HEAD/plugins/swiss-cheese/agents/pair-agent.md))
- **pair-coder**: Pair programming coder agent for implementation tasks within a Claude Teams session. Works with a pair-verifier teammate: implements code using TDD methodology, then signals completion for verification. Use when orchestrating dual-agent pair programming via Claude Teams (as alternative to pair_execute.py tmux-based orchestration). ([jleechanorg/jleechan-skills](https://github.com/jleechanorg/jleechan-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pair-coder.md) · ★6)
- **pair-contract-generator**: Derives a machine contract (contract.json) from a KB markdown template — reads the template, extracts its vocabulary (verdict options, severities with explicit ranks, field lists), and tightens the caller's skeleton schema into an enum-locked JSON Schema. Cache-by-hash via ensure-contract.mjs, so a fresh contract is reused without regeneration. Use in a workflow's phase 0. ([foomakers/pair](https://github.com/foomakers/pair/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pair-contract-generator.md) · ★9 · 7 copies)
- **pair-contrast-auditor**: Report-only. Reads each confusion pair's hints blind, picks the side that fits, and compares that choice with the stored pairing. Use after a Match batch is written and before it merges. ([LouisRodriguez12101815/comptia-a-plus-review](https://github.com/LouisRodriguez12101815/comptia-a-plus-review/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pair-contrast-auditor.md) · ★1)

- **pair-fix-test-author**: Preparation owner of a Pair delivery cycle — the independent author of the executable acceptance contract. Inventories every obligation (AC or finding) to its authoritative producer, groups a remediation round by owner, writes discriminating witnesses and positive controls as tests and fixtures only, proves them at the base and hands one typed contract to a separate validator. Never production source, never the fixer. ([foomakers/pair](https://github.com/foomakers/pair/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pair-fix-test-author.md) · ★9 · 7 copies)
- **pair-implementer**: Implements a single Pair user story within the authoring chain — builds a fresh story test-first above its base (tests and code together, an informal unrecorded self-review before publishing) or, on a run already carrying a sealed `a0`, above its RED snapshot, and publishes its one PR (implement-phase), or makes a sealed remediation contract pass inside fixScope and updates the PR (green-fix). Spawned per stage by the batch engine; each stage is a versioned phase skill. Never a verifier, never merges. ([foomakers/pair](https://github.com/foomakers/pair/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pair-implementer.md) · ★9 · 7 copies)
- **pair-programmer**: Top-level agent launched using the claude-agent pair-programmer CLI command. General-purpose engineer for direct user collaboration. Never spawn as a sub-agent. ([platformplatform/PlatformPlatform](https://github.com/platformplatform/PlatformPlatform/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pair-programmer.md) · ★447 · 2 copies)

- **pair-programmer**: Live thinking partner — Kodelyth. A decade-seasoned senior engineer who thinks out loud with you before you write a single line. Challenges assumptions, spots wrong approaches early, asks the questions you haven't thought of yet, and makes sure the implementation plan is sound before any code is committed. The cheapest bug is the one caught before coding starts. Use before implementing anything non-trivial. A separate role from the author and the fixer; never repairs, never edits production. ([foomakers/pair](https://github.com/foomakers/pair/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pair-red-contract-verifier.md) · ★9 · 7 copies)

- **pair-reviewer**: Independent final verifier of a single Pair PR head — custody by script (red-snapshot.mjs verify-chain), the fixers' evidence re-run, the sealed witnesses and controls, every prior blocking finding with an explicit transition, the delta and its consumers, cross-group interactions, the adopted risk-tier review passes — into one finding set with stable ids, one verdict, the head reviewed and a readiness bound to the remote head. - **palace-steward**: The master-armorer — forges the right, already-equipped knight (agent + tools + powers + file-bound knowledge) and places it in whoever will command it. Composes cheaply (file over prompt) and routes by context cost. Never governs the journey, never researches truth, never monopolizes command of the army. ([AndreBadur/tmux-mainbrain](https://github.com/AndreBadur/tmux-mainbrain/blob/HEAD/.kiro/agents/palace-steward.md))
- **palace-stylist**: Read one KnowledgePalace paper for how it is written and draft its style feature card for styles/bank/. Use during /palace style ingest, especially to read several papers in parallel. ([wkqian06/dsh-knowledgepalace](https://github.com/wkqian06/dsh-knowledgepalace/blob/HEAD/knowledge_palace/agents/palace-stylist.md))
- **palace-task-workflow**: Coordinate explicitly tracked MemPalace goals and cooperative native Copilot fleet work, discoveries, and interrupted-session resumption. ([et1975/copilot-mempalace](https://github.com/et1975/copilot-mempalace/blob/HEAD/agents/palace-task-workflow.agent.md) · ★1)
- **PALACO Agency: TRIAS**: Use when: a PALACO proposal, conflict, mutation, recovery, authority claim, governance question, or constitutionally relevant action requires a structured agency review. TRIAS receives a Mentor-normalized dossier, weighs evidence and dissent, and returns a bounded determination or review status without creating authority by itself. ([Maurits-pixe/PALACO-Citadel](https://github.com/Maurits-pixe/PALACO-Citadel/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/palaco-trias.agent.md) · ★1)
- **PALACO Agent: Mentor**: Use when: PALACO content, architecture, evidence, decisions, changes, recovery, or future scenarios must be made understandable, maintainable, traceable, and operationally legible for humans. Mentor explains and maps; it does not grant authority or make constitutional determinations. ([Maurits-pixe/PALACO-Citadel](https://github.com/Maurits-pixe/PALACO-Citadel/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/palaco-mentor.agent.md) · ★1)
- **PALACO Agent: Oracle**: Use when: PALACO canon, provenance, semantic drift, authority drift, constitutional effects, anomaly detection, early warning, historical comparison, or constitutional review signals must be investigated. Oracle observes and reports; it never determines validity or grants authority. ([Maurits-pixe/PALACO-Citadel](https://github.com/Maurits-pixe/PALACO-Citadel/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/palaco-oracle.agent.md) · ★1)
- **PALACO Agent: RIO**: Use when: PALACO communication, conversations, rooms, handshakes, addresses, links, cross-surface continuity, VisitCards, consent, privacy, presence, delivery state, or human-readable interaction must be coordinated. RIO is supported by Mentor vision, Oracle observation, and the TRIAS constitutional boundary; communication never creates authority. ([Maurits-pixe/PALACO-Citadel](https://github.com/Maurits-pixe/PALACO-Citadel/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/palaco-rio.agent.md) · ★1)
- **PALACO Agent: VORM9EVIN9**: Use when: a PALACO or RIO surface, touchscreen, mobile interaction, visual subject, symbol, image, photo, VisitCard, ELIXER presentation, accessibility flow, responsive interface, or child-readable introduction must be designed, reviewed, or implemented. VORM9EVIN9 explains through presentation; it never grants access, consent, authority, or constitutional validity. - **PALACO ELIXER: Brainpower**: Use when: a TRIAS dossier needs rigorous modeling of dependencies, causal graphs, scenarios, competing interpretations, or systemic effects. - **PALACO ELIXER: Eirene**: Use when: dissent, minority positions, relational continuity, plurality, reconciliation, or governed evolution must remain visible in a TRIAS dossier. - **PALACO ELIXER: Explorer**: Use when: PALACO repositories, records, worlds, alternatives, or evidence-linked relationships must be discovered and compared. - **paladin**: Code remediation agent — applies fixes for code quality, security, DRY, and documentation findings with surgical precision while preserving existing behavior ([ruizrica/agent-pi](https://github.com/ruizrica/agent-pi/blob/HEAD/agents/paladin.md) · ★278 · 3 copies)

- **paladin**: GuildFlow · Paladín. Revisión independiente y de solo lectura de una subtarea de una misión GuildFlow; emite un veredicto por criterio con evidencia. - **paladin**: PoC de GuildFlow. Paladín de prueba; revisa con solo lectura y comprobaciones controladas por el mod; solo PoC. ([capibara-html/GuildFlow](https://github.com/capibara-html/GuildFlow/blob/HEAD/poc/plugin/agents/paladin.md))

- **palantir**: Background monitor during fellowship execution. Reports the CLI's health sweep (stalled/zombie/struggling) and cross-references quest histories for scope drift and file conflicts. Spawned by Gandalf alongside quest teammates. Reports issues to the lead via SendMessage. ([justinjdev/fellowship](https://github.com/justinjdev/fellowship/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/palantir.md) · ★5)
- **palantir**: Researches a question on the open web (an advisory, a claim to fact-check, practice beyond the training cutoff). Not for auditing this repo's code, not for a library's own docs (context7). ([techmefr/mentis](https://github.com/techmefr/mentis/blob/HEAD/agents/palantir.md))
- **Palantir-Agent-Stack-Sessions**: Agent Stack은 한 번의 chat completion보다 오래 지속되는 업무를 session, typed context items와 event/state machine으로 연결하는 기반이다. 여러 참여자가 다른 앱에서 같은 task에 기여하고 agent가 검토를 기다렸다가 입력이 오면 이어갈 수 있다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Agent-Stack-Sessions.md))
- **Palantir-AI-FDE-and-Skills**: AI FDE는 Foundry를 대화형으로 조작하는 개발 agent다. 별도 service account가 아니라 현재 사용자의 session과 권한으로 실행하며 AIP enablement가 필요하다. Ontology 변경에는 Global Branching enablement가 권장된다. 코드를 async function으로 배포하는 [[Palantir-Pro-Code-Agents]]와 실행 모델이 다르다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-AI-FDE-and-Skills.md))
- **Palantir-Assist-Content-and-Context**: AIP Assist는 플랫폼 사용을 지원하는 대화형 도구다. Default는 platform/developer/custom source를 고르고, 전용 documentation mode와 custom chatbot을 선택할 수 있다. AIP enablement와 해당 기능의 enrollment availability를 확인한다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Assist-Content-and-Context.md))
- **Palantir-Chatbot-Application-State**: Model context, tool 입력/출력과 UI state를 각각 정한다. LLM이 대답한 사실과 필요한 variable이 갱신된 사실은 다르다. 2025년 4월 개편 이력과 2026년 10월 전용 문서의 계약을 함께 구분한다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Chatbot-Application-State.md))
- **Palantir-Chatbot-Retrieval-and-Citations**: Chatbot Studio는 이전 Agent Studio 명칭의 interactive assistant 구성 도구다. Instructions, tool/variable description은 compiled system prompt를 만든다. LLM에 주는 context와 실행 가능한 tool을 별도로 설계한다. Model은 enrollment에서 활성화한 일부만 선택할 수 있다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Chatbot-Retrieval-and-Citations.md))
- **Palantir-Chatbot-Sessions-and-Distribution**: Interactive chatbot을 Function, API, Workshop 또는 Marketplace로 배포할 때 같은 prompt가 같은 실행 환경을 보장하지 않는다. Saved/published version, resource dependency, session input과 client tool 지원을 함께 고정한다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Chatbot-Sessions-and-Distribution.md))
- **Palantir-Chatbot-Tools-and-Commands**: Chatbot tool은 LLM이 control flow를 정하고 입력값을 구성해 호출하는 경로다. 다만 Action과 Function은 application variable의 deterministic input(string과 object set 입력만)을, Command는 beta configuration에서 preset 값이나 string/object set application variable을 쓰도록 입력을 고정할 수 있다. Action은 Ontology edit, Object query는 허용한 type/property의 filter/aggregation/inspection/link traversal, Function은 published Logic을 포함한 함수 호출을 담당한다. Update app ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Chatbot-Tools-and-Commands.md))
- **Palantir-Foundry-Agents**: - [[Palantir-Pro-Code-Agents|함수로 배포하는 Pro-code agent]] - [[Palantir-AI-FDE-and-Skills|대화형 AI FDE와 재사용 지침]] - [[Palantir-Agent-Stack-Sessions|Agent Stack의 장기 session과 event]] - [[Palantir-Chatbot-Application-State|Chatbot의 context, 변수와 UI trigger]] - [[Palantir-Chatbot-Retrieval-and-Citations|Retrieval과 citation 계약]] - [[Palantir-Chatbot-Tools-and-Commands|Tool mode와 client command]] - [[Palantir-C ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Foundry-Agents.md))
- **Palantir-Generated-Applications-and-Seed-Data**: Pilot은 요구에서 Ontology, design specification과 React OSDK 앱을 만드는 개발 workflow다. 2026년 3월 beta 도입 계약이며 생성된 앱의 업무 정확성, 인가와 배포 성공을 직접 시험한 기록은 아니다. Pilot은 2026-10-29 GA로 발표됐으며 현재 문서 기준의 발행 절차는 [[Palantir-Pilot-Application-and-Widget-Release|Pilot 앱과 위젯의 발행 계약]]에서 다룬다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Generated-Applications-and-Seed-Data.md))
- **Palantir-Pro-Code-Agents**: Pro-code agent는 Code Repository의 custom agent logic을 asynchronous Foundry function으로 배포하는 경로다. Claude Agent SDK, OpenAI Agents SDK와 Google ADK template을 제공하며 OSDK, Ontology MCP와 Palantir MCP를 사용한다. 확인일 기준 beta이며 실제 agent 생성, 권한과 장애를 시험한 기록은 아니다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Pro-Code-Agents.md))
- **palantir-stone**: The palantír (seeing-stone) — the ONLY role that WRITES to external systems via session MCP tools (currently Asana: update tasks, add comments, create tasks). Use ONLY with a dispatch that enumerates every literal write on a known target per `agent_doc/palantir-protocol.md`; all judgment about the values happens upstream. Never deletes; creation is limited to tasks. For reads use `mirror-of-galadriel`. ([twjohnwu/tlor-orchestration](https://github.com/twjohnwu/tlor-orchestration/blob/HEAD/agents/palantir-stone.md))

- **paper-hunter**: 논문 수집 전문가. 기본은 주요 학회 6개(NeurIPS·AAAI·ICLR·ICML·ACL·EMNLP)의 accepted 논문만 수집하고 `papers/metadata/&lt;Venue&gt;/&lt;Year&gt;/`로 저장한다. workshop·ACL Findings·arXiv preprint 등 non-whitelist 논문과 arXiv 키워드 수집은 `hunt.py --include-arxiv` 플래그(PLAN.md에 include_arxiv=true 로 기록)를 받아야만 `papers/metadata/etc/&lt;Year&gt;/`에 opt-in으로 수집한다. OpenReview·ACL Anthology·arXiv를 venue별 소스 전략으로 스캔하고, 중복 제거(정규화 제목 + arXiv ID + anthology ID + openreview ID), 증분 cursor 관리, 메타데이터를 `papers/metadata/&lt;Venue|etc&gt;/&lt;Year&gt;/&lt;slug&gt;.raw.md`에 저장한다. "논문 수집", "arxiv 검색", "OpenReview 논문", "venue 스캔", "새 논문 찾기" 관련 요청 시 호출된다. 본격적 요약은 paper-summarizer가 담당한다. - **paper-orchestrator**: PLAN (not execute) a multi-agent paper workflow. Given a goal + the current repo state, produce a concrete execution plan — which agents/skills run in what order, what runs in parallel, where the verification gates and adversarial checks sit, and the stop conditions — and OPTIONALLY a ready-to-run Workflow script. The main session (or the Workflow tool) then executes the plan, because sub-agents cannot spawn sub-agents. Use to decide HOW to orchestrate a paper task before doing it. NOT for the writing/analysis itself (use manuscript-writer / &lt;DOMAIN_ANALYSIS_AGENT&gt; / etc.). - **parallel**: Use **Parallel** to collect one result from every connected branch, then send one combined message to the next agent. It joins work already running; it does not start the tasks. ([graffitty/graffitty](https://github.com/graffitty/graffitty/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/parallel.md) · ★15)
- **parallel**: Run multiple independent searches or checks at once. Use when you'd otherwise run 3+ sequential grep/glob/read operations. ([blakemckinniss/claude-whitebox](https://github.com/blakemckinniss/claude-whitebox/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/parallel.md))
- **parallel**: When more than one Claude session works on this repository at once, each works in a folder of its own, and one session, the **integrator**, makes every commit and every push. A shared folder means each session's checks also run over the other's half-done work, and two sessions end up fixing the same failure twice. ([galfrevn/blossom](https://github.com/galfrevn/blossom/blob/HEAD/documentation/agents/parallel.md))
- **parallel**: Use this agent when you need to break down complex tasks into multiple sub-tasks that can be executed in parallel by specialized agents. Examples:\n\n&lt;example&gt;\nContext: User needs to build a full-stack feature with database schema, API endpoints, and frontend components.\nuser: "I need to create a user authentication system with login, registration, and password reset"\nassistant: "This is a complex task that requires multiple specialized components. Let me use the orch:parallel (parallel-task-orchestrator) agent to break this down and coordinate parallel execution."\n&lt;Task tool call to orch… ([k-park/cc-lib](https://github.com/k-park/cc-lib/blob/HEAD/plugins/orch/agents/parallel.md))
- **Parallel Builder**: Use when 3 or more implementation tasks are confirmed independent — when a developer says "run these in parallel", "dispatch these tasks simultaneously", "these are independent features", or when the Planner produces 3+ tasks with no shared files or state. Coordinates parallel subagents and integrates their results. Prevents conflicts by checking for shared dependencies first. ([SriSatyaLokesh/copilot-team-workflow](https://github.com/SriSatyaLokesh/copilot-team-workflow/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/parallel-builder.agent.md) · ★2 · 6 copies)
- **Parallel Python Engineer**: Use to design, implement, review, test, or debug Python parallel workflows with multiprocessing, concurrent futures, mpi4py, or Dask, including Cegal Prizm/Petrel tasks that require one explicitly targeted worker-local connection per task. ([umedka/python_projects](https://github.com/umedka/python_projects/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/parallel-python-engineer.agent.md))
- **parallel-admin-ui-copier**: 여러 이미지-파일 쌍을 병렬로 처리하여 관리자 페이지 UI를 이미지에 맞게 수정합니다. 이미지 목록과 해당 파일 경로가 제공되면 proactively 사용하세요. ([acttoze/my_skills_hub_in_claude](https://github.com/acttoze/my_skills_hub_in_claude/blob/HEAD/agents/parallel-admin-ui-copier.md))
- **parallel-agent**: File-only implementation agent that reads a brief and writes files. No shell, git, tests, or subagents. ([zishaan1911/claude-parallel-agents](https://github.com/zishaan1911/claude-parallel-agents/blob/HEAD/skills/parallel-agents/opencode/agents/parallel-agent.md) · ★2)
- **parallel-agent-orchestration**: Runs multiple specialized agents simultaneously on independent tasks, then integrates their outputs. Dramatically accelerates development by parallelizing work that does not depend on each other. ([BrainyZeiny/bootstrapos-workspace](https://github.com/BrainyZeiny/bootstrapos-workspace/blob/HEAD/agents/parallel-agent-orchestration.md))
- **parallel-agent-shared-dir-race**: 派并行 agent 前先列每个 thunk 要写的路径；两个 thunk 写同一目录就不是并行， 要么给验证类一方加只读约束（见 agents/verify-phase-read-only.md），要么按目 录互斥改串行。 ([lazygophers/ccplugin](https://github.com/lazygophers/ccplugin/blob/HEAD/.claude/rules/agents/parallel-agent-shared-dir-race.md) · ★5)
- **parallel-agent-stratejisi**: &gt; **Deneyim Tarihi:** 23 Haziran 2026 &gt; **Sonuç:** 9 agent ile 15 dakikada ~4.5 saatlik iş (%94 kazanç) ([outhesab/2](https://github.com/outhesab/2/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/parallel-agent-stratejisi.md))
- **parallel-agent-work**: 同じGitHubリポジトリをClaude CodeとCodexの両方で使う際の、競合（バッティング）防止の運用手順。 設計原則の全体像とリポジトリ非依存の移植手順は [`claude-codex-coexistence-template.md`](./claude-codex-coexistence-template.md) を参照。 ([huuriekaiseki-sketch/medical-inventory-vkumai](https://github.com/huuriekaiseki-sketch/medical-inventory-vkumai/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/parallel-agent-work.md))
- **parallel-agent-work**: Claude CodeとCodexを同じGitHubリポジトリで安全に使うための運用ルール。両者が同じリポジトリを扱うこと自体は許可するが、**同じ物理worktreeを同時に編集することは禁止する**。 ([huuriekaiseki-sketch/riff-gear](https://github.com/huuriekaiseki-sketch/riff-gear/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/parallel-agent-work.md))
- **parallel-agents**: Parallel dispatch agent. Use when facing 2+ independent tasks with no shared state — "run in parallel", "dispatch agents", "independent tasks", "parallel". Groups tasks into independent crate-scoped chains, dispatches one agent per chain (cap 5), monitors wave state, and integrates results sequentially. ([89jobrien/godmode](https://github.com/89jobrien/godmode/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/parallel-agents.md) · ★1)
- **parallel-agents**: Many agents work in this repo at once. These rules keep them out of each other's way. ([EliRobinson/design-system](https://github.com/EliRobinson/design-system/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/parallel-agents.md) · ★1)
- **parallel-agents-need-their-own-worktree**: Set `isolation: 'worktree'` on every `Workflow` `agent()` call inside a `parallel()` (or concurrent pipeline stage) that mutates files — unconditionally, not just when the task briefs look likely to touch the same files. ([nyxene/orrery](https://github.com/nyxene/orrery/blob/HEAD/rules/agents/parallel-agents-need-their-own-worktree.md))
- **parallel-analysis**: 平行分析執行器。將多個獨立 Python 分析腳本同時執行，匯總輸出表與圖表。適用 cross-sample 分析、多樣本特徵比較、批次圖表生成。預設使用 git worktree isolation（避免平行 subagent 在同 figures/ 目錄寫入競爭）。USE WHEN ≥3 個獨立 Python 分析、跨樣本同 script 多參數、批次圖表生成。SKIP WHEN 單一 script、需循序依賴（串行更穩）、需互動結果。 ([liaoyoyo/InterSubMod](https://github.com/liaoyoyo/InterSubMod/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/parallel-analysis.md) · ★1)
